- Top-level functions:

  - mirror_step(a, p, steps): returns best candidate and score.

Mirror descent might be more stable for exact maximum. Let's design `mirror_optimize(a, deadline, max_iter?)`:
Input normalized a (sum=1), n, L. It returns best score/a.
Algorithm:
- best_a, best_sc.
- loga = log(a + eps). But a can be zero; use eps 1e-300? For stable, store loga = log(a) with a set to 1e-300 for zeros. Updating loga can go negative/positive. To reconstruct: m=max(loga); a=exp(loga-m); S=a.sum(); a/=S. If all exp underflow? m finite. Good.
- Use time limit. For each beta in schedule [10, 50, 200, 1000, 5000, 20000, 100000, 500000? maybe depend n]. Since h scale ~1/n², beta times c diff: c diff ~M - lower, maybe >1/n? To focus on top, beta ~ n^2? For n~4000, n²=16e6. But LSE with beta huge may underflow; we shift. Beta can be 1e5-1e7. We'll schedule up to max 5e6, clip. But gradient may be noisier.
- At each beta, run a number of iterations until no improvement or time. Use adaptive alpha.
Iteration:
  a = reconstruct from loga.
  c=conv(a); cm=c.max(); exact=2n cm.
  update best.
  p via softmax of beta*(c-cm) (if beta huge and c-cm negative large -> exp 0; at least max one). Use stable.
  h = grad_h(a,p).
  d = h - h.mean(). Could add tiny ridge d += 1e-12.
  d_norm = max(abs(d).max() or norm). We want step with normalized direction. Use d_scale = d.max()-d.min() maybe. Candidate alphas = base * [1,2,3,5,7,10, 0.5...]. Base initially maybe 0.1? Since d normalized to max range 1, update loga -= alpha*d (with d in [-something, something]). If d max range=1, alpha=0.1 changes weights by e^0.1 ~10%. Could be okay. We can search over [1e-4,1e-3,1e-2,0.1,0.3,1.0]. Evaluate exact score by reconstructing and conv. Choose best alpha. If best == current (no improvement), reduce alpha_max. If improvement, increase maybe.
  Use momentum? Could keep previous direction v = 0.7 v + d. But normalization? Maybe not.
  Since we use exact max score for candidate, we can accept any improvement. This is like forward step in simplex with mirror.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=..., budget_s=2400):
  t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-30
  best_a=None; best_score=inf
  # collect starts
  starts=[] with labels
  if parent: parent (orig length), resampled to n_parent? already
     maybe mirror, reverse, sym, shuffle? We'll process parent main separately.
  add uniform for n in [1024,2048,4096,8192] maybe if no parent or extra
  add periodic starts for n_parent or 4096
  add noisy starts
  # Phase 0: Quick evaluate and short mirror for starts until 30% budget
  for start in starts (time limited):
     a=normalize(start); sc=score(a); update
     if time remaining: short_mirror(a, time_limit=..., beta_schedule_short)
     update
  # Phase 1: Long mirror on best with different lengths/betas until 60%
  long_mirror(best_a, remaining fraction)
  # Phase 2: L-BFGS polish on best and maybe best few with beta schedule until 85%
  for beta in [100,500,2000,10000,50000]: run_lsys with time proportion, correct grad, penalty; update
  # Phase 3: Additional starts/restarts from best with perturbations + quick L-BFGS/mirror until deadline-60
  # Phase 4: Final coordinate/n side search maybe.
  return best_a
```

`lbfgs_objective`:
```
a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
n=a.size
S=a.sum()
if S<1e-8: return 1e10, zeros
c=full_conv(a, L) # length maybe c
maxc=c.max()
scaled=beta*(c-maxc); scaled=np.clip(scaled, -50, 50); e=np.exp(scaled); s=e.sum()
L=maxc+np.log(s)/beta
F0=2*n*L/(S*S)
pen=1e-3*(S-1)**2
val=F0+pen
if s==0: grad=zeros
else p=e/s
h=grad_h(a,p,Lh)
# ∂F0 = 2n(2h/S^2 - 2L/S^3)
g=4*n*(h/(S*S) - L/(S**3)) + 2*penalty*(S-1)
return val,g
```
For `minimize`, we pass initial a sum=1, bounds [(0,1000)]*n. Use options maxiter maybe based time. But L-BFGS with p-second grad returns array each; scale. Tolerance gtol 1e-12. After result normalize, exact score. The exact objective is max not LSE, so L-BFGS may stop at smooth local. Good.

Mirror optimization implementation details:
```
def mirror_phase(a, deadline, beta_list, alpha, max_iter_phase):
   a=normalize(a); n; L; Lh
   loga=np.log(a+1e-300)
   best_a=a; best_sc=score(a)
   for beta in beta_list:
      for it in range(...):
        if time>deadline: break
        a=reconstruct(loga)
        c=full_conv(a)
        cm=c.max(); sc=2*n*cm
        if sc<best_sc: update
        # if cm <= lower bound? break
        p=lse_weights(c,beta)
        h=grad_h(a,p)
        d=h - h.mean()
        dmax=np.max(np.abs(d)); if dmax<1e-15: # try LSE with smaller beta? break
        d=d/dmax
        # candidates
        cand_alphas = np.unique([alpha*0.25, alpha*0.5, alpha, alpha*2, alpha*4])
        best_alpha=alpha
        base_loga=loga.copy()
        improved=False
        for al in cand:
            loga2=base_loga - al*d
            a2=reconstruct(loga2)
            sc2=score(a2)
            if sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc2; best_a=a2; loga=loga2; best_alpha=al; improved=True
        # after loop, if improved and best_alpha>alpha: alpha=min(best_alpha*1.2 if chosen high?; else reduce
        if not improved:
             alpha *= 0.5
             if alpha<1e-6: break
        else:
             # if best_alpha is 4*alpha, increase a bit
             if best_alpha > alpha*1.5: alpha = min(best_alpha, alpha*1.1)
             elif best_alpha < alpha*0.7: alpha=best_alpha
   return best_a,best_sc
```
But note if multiple candidates improve, we should choose the one with best score, not just any. Good.

Reconstruct function for loga should be top-level:
```
def exp_normalize(loga):
   m=loga.max()
   v=np.exp(loga-m)
   s=v.sum()
   if s<=0: return zeros? uniform
   return v/s
```
But updating loga from normalized a: If we reconstruct with normalization, loga no longer represents actual normalized a (it was normalized away from loga). If we do loga -= αd, then reconstruct normalizes; the implicit mean of log changes. This is okay: loga is up to additive constant. Let's outline helper functions in code:

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def next_pow2(m):
    return 1 << (max(1, m)-1).bit_length()

def full_conv(a, L=None):
    n=a.size; if L is None: L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
    # tiny negatives
    # c[c<0]=0? only if negative small
    c = np.where(c>0, c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Should we clip? If c has real small negative in high values due numerical? Convolution nonnegative; negative only tiny at zeros. Clipping to 0 okay. But if FFT roundoff negative larger? Not. Use `np.maximum(c,0,out=c)`.

`full_conv_no_clip`? Not needed.

`score_c(a,c)`:
```
S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12: return np.inf,
return 2.0*a.size*np.max(c)/(S*S)
```
`exact_score(a)` compute c.

`normalize(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a, float).ravel()
a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
S=a.sum()
if S<1e-15: return np.ones_like(a)/len(a) if len>0 else np.array([1.0])
return a/S
```

`resample(a,n)`: if len=1? `x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(a)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a); return normalize(out)`.

`trig_start(n,q,c,phi,floor)`:
```
x=np.arange(n)
a=1.0 + c*np.cos(2*np.pi*q*x/n + phi)
a=np.maximum(a, floor)
return normalize(a)
```
Need top-level no lambda.

`noise_start(n,rng,smooth_sigma)`:
```
y=rng.standard_normal(n)
L=next_pow2(n)
Y=np.fft.rfft(y,L)
freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(L)*L
mask=np.exp(-(freq/(L*smooth_sigma))**2) # maybe
Y*=mask
z=np.fft.irfft(Y,L)[:n]
z /= (np.std(z)+1e-12)
a=1.0 + 0.6*z
a=np.maximum(a,1e-3)
return normalize(a)
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-45.0
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    # parent
    parr=None
    try:
      hs=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
      if hs is not None:
        arr=np.asarray(hs,dtype=float).ravel()
        if arr.size>=16 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
           arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
           parr=arr
    except Exception: pass
    candidates=[]  # list of (label, a, short_time)
    if parr is not None:
        # main parent original if reasonable
        n_main = parr.size
        if not (n_main == 0):
           a0=normalize(parr); sc=score; update; candidates.append(( 'parent', a0, 60? ))
           # parent resampled to power of two? maybe if n_main not too small
           for n_power in [1024,2048,4096,8192]:
               if n_power != n_main:
                  a=normalize(resample(a0,n_power)); sc=score; update candidates list
        else...
        # sym? rev same score; no need evaluate rev but maybe optimize? skip
        # shuffled parent
        a=normalize(parr[rng.permutation(parr.size)]); candidates append limited
    else:
        n=4096; a=... 
    # Add trig starts for n_main or 4096
    n_start = parent length if 1024<=n<=20000 else 4096
    for q in [2,4,8,16]: for c in [0.3,0.8]: for phi in 2 rng: add if time
    # Add noise start
```

Let's detail `mirror_optimize` code with no closures:
```
def mirror_optimize(a, seconds, rng=None, betas=None, alpha0=0.05, tag=""):
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+float(seconds)
    a=normalize(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<4: return a, exact_score(a)
    loga=np.log(a+1e-300)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a)
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng()
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1); Lh=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    if betas is None:
       beta_max = int(max(20000, min(5000000, max(1, n)*n/20)))
       base=[10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000,500000,2000000,10000000]
       betas=[b for b in base if b<=beta_max*2]
       if not betas: betas=[100,1000,10000]
    alpha=alpha0
    stall=0
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic()>=t_end: break
       for it in range(1000000):
          if time.monotonic()>=t_end: break
          # reconstruct
          a=exp_normalize(loga)
          c=full_conv(a)
          cm=float(c.max())
          sc=2.0*n*cm
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
              best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
          else:
              stall += 1
          if stall > 300:
              # perturb loga if have rng
              loga = loga + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
              stall=0
              continue
          # weights
          scaled=beta*(c-cm); scaled=np.clip(scaled, -50, 0)
          e=np.exp(scaled); s=e.sum(); p=e/s
          h=grad_h(a,p,Lh)
          # Add small regularization to avoid exact constant? Maybe use p over lower with tolerance already beta.
          hh=h - np.mean(h)
          dm=np.max(np.abs(hh))
          if dm < 1e-14:
              # add tiny noise direction from c? Or next p with lower beta? break to next beta
              break
          d=hh/dm
          # candidates
          cands=(alpha*0.333, alpha, alpha*3.0)
          base_loga=loga
          best_alpha=alpha; best_cand_sc=sc; best_cand_loga=None; best_cand_a=None
          for al in cands:
              lo=base_loga - al*d
              a2=exp_normalize(lo)
              sc2=exact_score(a2)
              if sc2 < best_cand_sc - 1e-15:
                  best_cand_sc=sc2; best_alpha=al; best_cand_loga=lo; best_cand_a=a2
          if best_cand_loga is not None:
              loga=best_cand_loga; a=best_cand_a
              # Re-center loga to avoid large
              loga=loga - loga.mean()
              # adapt alpha
              if best_alpha > alpha*1.5:
                  alpha=min(1.0, best_alpha*1.05)
              elif best_alpha < alpha*0.7:
                  alpha=max(1e-6, best_alpha)
          else:
              alpha *= 0.5
              if alpha < 1e-6:
                  break
    # final evaluate
    a=exp_normalize(loga); sc=exact_score(a)
    if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a
    return best_a,best_sc
```
This uses `exact_score` which computes full conv. For candidate evaluation, 3 recovs. Could be heavy but okay.

L-BFGS after mirror:
```
def run_lbfgs_polish(a, maxseconds, beta, pen=1e-4, t0_end=None):
   a=normalize(a); n=a.size
   sc=exact_score(a)
   if n>20000 or maxseconds<1: return a,sc
   remaining = max(10, maxseconds)
   maxiter=int(min(20000, max(500, remaining* (400 if n<6000 else 150))))
   bounds=[(0,1000)]*n
   res=minimize(lbfgs_objective, a, args=(beta, pen), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxiter*2,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':50})
   out=normalize(np.clip(res.x,0,1000))
   sc2=exact_score(out)
   return out if sc2<sc? For main update outside.
```
But `minimize` may take longer than maxseconds. We can't abort. We can estimate by first doing a small trial? Or limit maxiter small (2000). Since time budget large, okay. However if n=10000, one grad maybe 10ms, 2000=20s. Fine.

`lbfgs_objective` top-level:
```
def lbfgs_objective(a,beta,penalty):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12:
       return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    c=full_conv(a)
    cm=float(c.max())
    scaled=beta*(c-cm); scaled=np.clip(scaled, -50, 0)
    e=np.exp(scaled); se=e.sum()
    if se<=0:
       L=cm
       p=np.full(2*n-1,1.0/(2*n-1))
    else:
       L=cm + float(np.log(se))/beta
       p=e/se
    F0=2.0*n*L/(S*S)
    val=F0+penalty*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
    h=grad_h(a,p)  # Lh computed inside
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    g=4.0*n*(h/S2 - L/S3) + 2.0*penalty*(S-1.0)
    return val, g
```
Need `grad_h` uses Lh; for L-BFGS beta with a not normalized, p based on c normalized? p same. h scale with a. Good.

Let's think of possible bugs in `full_conv`: For n=1, 2n-1=1, L=1, A*A, irfft length1: if a[0]^2? np.fft.irfft of length1? Should work. But n large.

`next_pow2(m)`: if m<=1 return1. Using bit_length: `1 << (m-1).bit_length()` for m>0. Good.

`grad_h(a,p,Lh=None)`: If Lh None compute. We can precompute Lh in mirror and L-BFGS? L-BFGS objective computes each time; okay. To avoid repeated `a[::-1]` copy? We can `ar=a[::-1]` view. p length 2n-1. `Mr=next_pow2(3*n-2)` maybe. For h we need length n + len(p) -1 = n+2n-1-1=3n-2. Good.
```
def grad_h(a,p,Lh=None):
   n=a.size
   if Lh is None: Lh=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   Ar=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Lh)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p, Lh)
   h=np.fft.irfft(Ar*P,Lh)
   h=h[n-1:2*n-1]
   # remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
   return h
```
Let's test with p length 2n-1; rfft pads zeros to Lh. Good.

Let's write top-level start functions:
```
def trig_start(n, q, amp, phase, floor=1e-4):
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    a=1.0+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*q*x/float(n)+phase)
    a=np.maximum(a,floor)
    return normalize(a)

def noise_start(n, rng, level=0.5, sigma_frac=0.2):
    y=rng.standard_normal(n)
    L=next_pow2(n)
    Y=np.fft.rfft(y,L)
    freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(L)*L
    sigma=n*sigma_frac
    Y *= np.exp(-0.5*(freq/sigma)**2)
    z=np.fft.irfft(Y,L)[:n]
    std=float(np.std(z))
    if std<1e-12: z=np.ones(n)
    else: z=z/std
    a=1.0+level*z
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-4)
    return normalize(a)

def shuffled_start(a, rng):
    a=normalize(a); n=a.size
    if n<2: return a
    return normalize(a[rng.permutation(n)])
```
For `trig_start`, q should be integer maybe use `q` int. The sequence periodic with q cycles over length; endpoints maybe discontinuous if q not integer? q integer, f(0)=1+amp cos phase, f(n-1) different; no boundary condition in linear convolution. Good. q=1 gave bad maybe; q high maybe. Use q in [2,3,5,8,12,16]. Amp 0.5,0.9.

Maybe use sine instead of cos? Phase covers.

Let's think of exact `norm` and `score` functions:
```
def normalize_sequence(a):
   a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s < 1e-15:
       return np.full(a.size, 1.0/float(a.size))
   a=a/s
   return a
```
`full_convolution(a, L=None)`:
```
n=int(a.size); target=2*n-1
if L is None: L=next_pow2(target)
A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:target]
# due to roundoff, c may have small negative
c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
return c
```
Note if a has large sum not normalized, c can be huge; clipping negatives fine.

`exact_score(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
s=float(a.sum())
if s<1e-15: return np.inf
c=full_convolution(a)
m=float(c.max())
return float(2.0*a.size*m/(s*s))
```
For normalized, returns. For speed in mirror, we already have c and a normalized; use `score_from_c(a,c)` maybe.
```
def score_from_c(a,c):
   s=float(a.sum()); n=a.size; if s<...; return 2*n*max(c)/s²
```
Mirror uses `score_from_c(a,c)` instead of exact_score to avoid recompute conv. Candidate uses exact_score. Add top-level.

`grad_h(a,p,Lh=None)`:
```
n=a.size
if Lh is None: Lh=next_pow2(3*n-2)
ar=a[::-1]
Ar=np.fft.rfft(ar, Lh)
P=np.fft.rfft(p, Lh)
h=np.fft.irfft(Ar*P, Lh)
h=h[n-1:2*n-1]
# due roundoff negative tiny; h can be negative small; maybe leave (gradient) but for stability clip? If clip, gradient direction slightly. Use np.maximum(h,0, out=h) because p,a nonnegative; small negative noise.
np.maximum(h, 0.0, out=h)
return h
```
Clipping negative to 0 can make h mean nonnegative, okay. But if large negative due numerical? not.

`lse_weights(c,beta)`:
```
cm=float(c.max())
if beta <= 0:
   tol=max(1e-12, cm*1e-3)
   w=np.where(c >= cm - tol, 1.0, 0.0)
   s=w.sum(); if s==0: w=np.array([1.0,0...])
   return w/s
scaled=beta*(c-cm)
scaled=np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0)
e=np.exp(scaled)
s=e.sum()
if s<=0: p=np.ones_like(c); p[0]=1; return p
return e/s
```
If beta large but no near, p at max. Good.

`exp_normalize_log(loga)`:
```
m=float(loga.max())
v=np.exp(loga - m)
s=float(v.sum())
if s <= 1e-300 or not finite: return uniform
return v/s
```
Need if loga contains nan? Should not. If m is inf? loga finite. If some loga very negative, exp 0. If m very negative? okay. Could periodically recenter after normalization. Good.

Now, `mirror_optimize` stage time:
```
def mirror_optimize(a, seconds, rng=None, betas=None, weights=None, alpha0=0.2, tag="mirror", stall_limit=250):
    t0=time.monotonic(); total=float(max(0,seconds)); t_end=t0+total
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    n=a.size
    sc=exact_score(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
    if total <= 1 or n<4: return best_a,best_sc
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng()
    if betas is None: ... generate with max_beta maybe. weights similarly.
    # ensure weights length
    total_w=sum(weights)
    loga=np.log(a + 1e-300)
    loga -= loga.mean()
    Lh=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    alpha=alpha0
    perturb=0
    stage_start=time.monotonic()
    for bi,beta in enumerate(betas):
       if time.monotonic()>=t_end: break
       stage_seconds = total * weights[bi] / total_w
       t_stage = max(time.monotonic()+stage_seconds, time.monotonic()+1.0)
       if tag: print(f"  {tag} beta={beta:.0f} stage~{stage_seconds:.0f}s")
       beta_cur=float(beta)
       stall=0
       while time.monotonic() < t_stage and time.monotonic() < t_end:
           a=exp_normalize_log(loga)
           c=full_convolution(a)
           cm=float(c.max())
           sc=2.0*n*cm  # since sum normalized but compute S just in case
           # Actually a sum =1, but due exp normalization. Use score_from_c? exact with s=1.
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
               best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0; perturb=0
           else:
               stall += 1
           if stall > stall_limit:
               if beta_cur > 50.0:
                   beta_cur = max(20.0, beta_cur*0.25)
                   stall=0
               else:
                   loga = loga + 0.04*rng.standard_normal(n)
                   loga -= loga.mean()
                   perturb += 1
                   stall=0
                   if perturb > 50:
                       break
           p = lse_weights(c, beta_cur)
           h=grad_h(a,p,Lh)
           hc = h - float(np.mean(h))
           maxh = float(np.max(np.abs(hc)))
           if maxh < 1e-13:
               # cannot move with this p; lower beta or perturb
               if beta_cur > 50:
                   beta_cur = max(20.0, beta_cur*0.25)
                   continue
               else:
                   loga = loga + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
                   loga -= loga.mean(); stall=0
                   continue
           d=hc / maxh
           # candidate alphas
           cals = (alpha*0.333, alpha, alpha*3.0)
           base_loga=loga
           best_al=alpha; best_cand_sc=sc; best_loga=None; best_a2=None
           for al in cals:
               if al <= 0: continue
               log2=base_loga - al*d
               a2=exp_normalize_log(log2)
               sc2=exact_score(a2)
               if sc2 < best_cand_sc - 1e-15:
                   best_cand_sc=sc2; best_al=al; best_loga=log2; best_a2=a2
           if best_loga is not None:
               loga=best_loga - best_loga.mean()
               # alpha adapt
               if best_al > alpha * 1.5:
                   alpha = min(1.2, best_al * 1.05)
               elif best_al < alpha * 0.7:
                   alpha = max(1e-6, best_al)
           else:
               alpha *= 0.5
               if alpha < 1e-6:
                   break
    a=exp_normalize_log(loga); sc=exact_score(a)
    if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a
    if tag: print(f"  {tag} done score={best_sc:.10f}")
    return best_a,best_sc
```
Note: In mirror, `sc=2.0*n*cm` assumes sum=1. `a` from exp_normalize sum=1. Good. Candidate `exact_score` recomputes sum; fine.

But candidate `exact_score` re-clips and normalizes? It doesn't normalize, just uses sum. Since a2 normalized. Good.

Potential issue: `exact_score` called for candidates uses full conv, but c for current a already computed. We can use `score_from_c` for current sc.

`stall_limit` high; if lowest beta and perturbation many, break stage. Fine.

def default_mirror_betas(n):
    beta_max = int(max(5e5, min(2e7, 4*float(n)*float(n))))
    base=[20,100,500,2500,10000,50000,200000,1000000,5000000,20000000]
    base_w=[1,1,2,2,3,4,6,8,10,12]
    betas=[]; ws=[]
    for b,w in zip(base,base_w):
       if b <= beta_max or b == base[-1]:
          betas.append(b); ws.append(w)
    if not betas: return [100,1000], [1,1]
    return betas, ws
```

Now, L-BFGS top-level `run_lbfgs_limit(a, beta, seconds, penalty=1e-4)`:
```
def run_lbfgs_limit(a,beta,seconds,penalty=1e-4):
    t_start=time.monotonic(); a=normalize_sequence(a); sc=exact_score(a); n=a.size
    if n<4 or seconds<2: return a,sc
    if n > 20000: return a,sc
    remaining=max(1.0, min(600.0,(seconds - (time.monotonic()-t_start))))
    # estimate iterator per sec: 30 if n<6000 else 10
    per_sec = 40 if n < 6000 else (20 if n < 12000 else 8)
    maxiter=int(min(10000, max(200, int(remaining*per_sec))))
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)] for _ in range(n)]  # cannot use lambda? list comprehension okay no lambda.
    try:
       res=minimize(lbfgs_objective, a, args=(beta, penalty), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxiter*3,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':50})
       out=normalize_sequence(np.clip(res.x,0,1000))
       sc2=exact_score(out)
       if sc2 < sc: return out,sc2
    except Exception as e:
       print("lbfgs exception",e)
    return a,sc
```
List comprehension `[(0.0,1000.0)] * n` better, no loop. `bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n`. Good.

`lbfgs_objective` must not print. Use top-level.

Potential memory if n=20000 bounds list large but okay. Time estimate per sec maybe too high. To be safe, maxiter = min(3000, ...). The final improvement maybe needs few. Use `min(5000, ...)`. If n=8192, remaining 200s, per_sec15 =>3000 cap; maybe 30s. Good.

But L-BFGS with maxiter 3000 and n=8192 each objective maybe 5ms ->15s; line search maybe 2x ->30s. okay.

def add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, sc, label, a):
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return row_map, best_sc, best_a
    prev = row_map.get(label)
    if prev is None or sc < prev[0]:
        row_map[label] = (sc, a.copy())
    if sc < best_sc:
        print(f"NEW BEST {sc:.10f} len={len(a)} label={label}")
        return row_map, sc, a.copy()
    return row_map, best_sc, best_a

Now, let's consider if `mirror_optimize`'s default `betas` weights are top-level. We'll implement `default_mirror_settings(n)` returning betas, weights. It uses no closures.

```
def default_mirror_settings(n):
    beta_max = int(max(200000.0, min(5000000.0, 4.0*float(n)*float(n))))
    base = [20,100,500,2500,10000,50000,200000,1000000,5000000]
    ww = [1,1,2,2,3,4,6,8,10]
    betas=[]; ws=[]
    for i,b in enumerate(base):
       if b <= beta_max:
          betas.append(b); ws.append(ww[i])
    if len(betas) < 2:
       return [50,500], [1,1]
    return betas, ws
```

```python
"""
Improve the previous step-function construction by combining:
- corrected LSE convolution gradients,
- mirror-descent / multiplicative weight updates in the simplex,
- adaptive beta schedules and top-peak active-set smoothing,
- several asymmetric/resampled/noisy restarts to escape symmetric local minima,
- L-BFGS polishing with a scale penalty.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sequence(a):
    """Clip, make nonnegative, finite, and normalize the sum to 1."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    return a


def resample_sequence(a, n_out):
    """Linear resample a normalized sequence to a new length and renormalize."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n_out = int(max(1, n_out))
    n_in = int(a.size)
    if n_in == n_out:
        return a.copy()
    if n_in == 1:
        return np.full(n_out, 1.0 / float(n_out), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_out if n_out == 1 else n_in, dtype=np.float64)
    if n_in == 1:
        x_old = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_in, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_out, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_sequence(out)


def full_convolution(a, L=None):
    """Full nonnegative linear convolution a*a, computed by FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    target = 2 * n - 1
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2(target)
    L = int(max(L, target))
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:target]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def score_from_c(a, c):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    c = full_convolution(a)
    return score_from_c(a, c)


def grad_h(a, p, Lh=None):
    """
    For L = LSE(c), return h such that dL/d(a_j) = 2 h_j.
    h_j = sum_l a_l p_{j+l}.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if Lh is None:
        Lh = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Lh)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, Lh)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Ar * P, Lh)
    h = h[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(h, 0.0, out=h)
    return h


def lse_weights(c, beta):
    """Soft-weights for convex $L_\\beta$ approximation to max(c)."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if cm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cm):
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        p[0] = 1.0
        return p
    if beta <= 0.0:
        tol = max(1e-12, 1e-4 * cm)
        p = np.where(c >= cm - tol, 1.0, 0.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            return p
        return p / s
    scaled = beta * (c - cm)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -30.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    return e / s


def exp_normalize_log(loga):
    """Convert a log-amplitude vector to a normalized probability vector."""
    loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(loga)):
        loga = np.where(np.isfinite(loga), loga, -700.0)
    m = float(loga.max())
    v = np.exp(loga - m)
    s = float(v.sum())
    n = int(v.size)
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return v / s


def trig_start(n, q, amp, phase, floor=1e-4):
    """Bounded trigonometric nonnegative start."""
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(q) * x / float(n) + float(phase))
    a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def noise_start(n, rng, level=0.55, sigma_frac=0.18):
    """Smooth positive random start."""
    n = int(max(2, n))
    y = rng.standard_normal(n)
    L = next_pow2(n)
    Y = np.fft.rfft(y, L)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(L) * float(L)
    sigma = max(1.0, float(n) * float(sigma_frac))
    Y *= np.exp(-0.5 * (freq / sigma) ** 2)
    z = np.fft.irfft(Y, L)[:n]
    std = float(np.std(z))
    if std < 1e-12:
        z = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        z = z / std
    a = 1.0 + float(level) * z
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-4)
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def shuffled_start(a, rng):
    """Random permutation restart, preserving the multiset of heights."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 2:
        return a
    return normalize_sequence(a[rng.permutation(n)])


def asymmetric_start(a, rng):
    """Mix with reversed sequence using a random non-1/2 weight."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    r = float(rng.uniform(0.18, 0.82))
    b = (1.0 - r) * a + r * a[::-1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return normalize_sequence(b)


def default_mirror_settings(n):
    n = float(max(4, n))
    beta_max = int(max(200000.0, min(5000000.0, 4.0 * n * n)))
    base = [20, 100, 500, 2500, 10000, 50000, 200000, 1000000, 5000000]
    ww = [1, 1, 2, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 10]
    betas = []
    weights = []
    for b, w in zip(base, ww):
        if b <= beta_max:
            betas.append(float(b))
            weights.append(float(w))
    if len(betas) < 2:
        return [50.0, 500.0, 5000.0], [1.0, 1.0, 2.0]
    return betas, weights


def quick_mirror_settings(n):
    n = float(max(4, n))
    beta_max = int(max(2000.0, min(300000.0, float(n) * n / 8.0)))
    base = [50, 500, 5000, 50000, 300000]
    ww = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
    betas = []
    weights = []
    for b, w in zip(base, ww):
        if b <= beta_max:
            betas.append(float(b))
            weights.append(float(w))
    if len(betas) < 2:
        return [50.0, 500.0, 5000.0], [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]
    return betas, weights


def high_mirror_settings(n):
    n = float(max(4, n))
    beta_max = int(max(1000000.0, min(8000000.0, 6.0 * n * n)))
    base = [500000, 1000000, 3000000, 8000000]
    ww = [1, 2, 4, 6]
    betas = []
    weights = []
    for b, w in zip(base, ww):
        if b <= beta_max:
            betas.append(float(b))
            weights.append(float(w))
    if len(betas) < 2:
        return [1000000.0, 3000000.0], [1.0, 2.0]
    return betas, weights


def mirror_optimize(a, seconds, rng=None, betas=None, weights=None, alpha0=0.2,
                    tag="mirror", stall_limit=150):
    """
    Multiplicative/mirror-descent optimization of the smooth LSE objective.
    The direction is the centered LSE gradient in a-space; log-scaling keeps
    nonnegativity and normalization natural.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(max(0.0, seconds))
    t_end = t0 + total

    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    if total <= 1.0 or n < 4:
        return best_a, best_sc

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng()

    if betas is None or len(betas) == 0:
        betas, weights = default_mirror_settings(n)
    betas = [float(b) for b in betas]
    if weights is None:
        weights = [1.0] * len(betas)
    weights = [float(w) for w in weights]
    if len(weights) != len(betas):
        m = min(len(weights), len(betas))
        weights = weights[:m]
        betas = betas[:m]
    total_w = float(sum(weights))
    if total_w <= 0.0:
        total_w = 1.0
        weights = [1.0 / float(len(weights))] * len(weights)

    Lc = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Lh = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300, out=a.astype(np.float64).copy())
    loga = loga - float(loga.mean())

    alpha = float(max(1e-6, alpha0))
    perturb_rounds = 0

    for bi, beta_level in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() >= t_end:
            break
        stage_seconds = total * weights[bi] / total_w
        stage_seconds = max(1.0, stage_seconds)
        t_stage = max(time.monotonic() + stage_seconds, t_end)
        if tag:
            print(f"    {tag}: beta={beta_level:.0f}, stage~{stage_seconds:.0f}s")

        beta_cur = float(beta_level)
        stall = 0
        low_beta = 20.0

        while time.monotonic() < t_stage and time.monotonic() < t_end:
            a = exp_normalize_log(loga)
            c = full_convolution(a, Lc)
            sc = score_from_c(a, c)

            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                stall = 0
                perturb_rounds = 0
            else:
                stall += 1

            if stall > stall_limit:
                if beta_cur > low_beta:
                    beta_cur = max(low_beta, beta_cur * 0.25)
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    loga = loga + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                    loga = loga - float(loga.mean())
                    perturb_rounds += 1
                    stall = 0
                    if perturb_rounds > 60:
                        break

            p = lse_weights(c, beta_cur)
            h = grad_h(a, p, Lh)
            hc = h - float(h.mean())
            maxh = float(np.max(np.abs(hc))) if hc.size else 0.0

            if maxh < 1e-13:
                if beta_cur > low_beta:
                    beta_cur = max(low_beta, beta_cur * 0.25)
                    continue
                loga = loga + 0.06 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                loga = loga - float(loga.mean())
                stall = 0
                perturb_rounds += 1
                if perturb_rounds > 60:
                    break

            d = hc / maxh

            cals = (alpha * 0.333, alpha, alpha * 3.0)
            base_loga = loga
            best_al = alpha
            best_cand_sc = sc
            best_loga = None
            best_a2 = None

            for al in cals:
                al = float(min(2.0, max(1e-6, al)))
                log2 = base_loga - al * d
                a2 = exp_normalize_log(log2)
                c2 = full_convolution(a2, Lc)
                sc2 = score_from_c(a2, c2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_cand_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_cand_sc = sc2
                    best_al = al
                    best_loga = log2
                    best_a2 = a2

            if best_loga is not None:
                loga = best_loga - float(best_loga.mean())
                if best_al > alpha * 1.5:
                    alpha = min(1.2, best_al * 1.05)
                elif best_al < alpha * 0.7:
                    alpha = max(1e-6, best_al)
            else:
                alpha *= 0.5
                if alpha < 1e-6:
                    break

    a = exp_normalize_log(loga)
    c = full_convolution(a, Lc)
    sc = score_from_c(a, c)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        best_a = a.copy()

    if tag:
        print(f"    {tag}: finished score={best_sc:.10f}")
    return best_a, best_sc


def lbfgs_objective(a, beta, penalty):
    """Smooth LSE objective with a mild sum(a)=1 penalty and exact LSE gradient."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = full_convolution(a)
    cm = float(c.max())
    scaled = beta * (c - cm)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -30.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        L = cm
        p = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        L = cm + float(np.log(se)) / beta
        p = e / se

    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    F0 = 2.0 * float(n) * L / S2
    pen = float(penalty) * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)
    val = F0 + pen

    h = grad_h(a, p)
    g = 4.0 * float(n) * (h / S2 - L / S3)
    g += 2.0 * float(penalty) * (S - 1.0)
    return float(val), g


def run_lbfgs_limit(a, beta, seconds, penalty=1e-4):
    """Short L-BFGS-B polish with a safe iteration budget."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    if n < 4 or seconds < 2.0 or n > 20000:
        return best_a, best_sc

    # Conservative rough cost model.
    if n < 6000:
        per_sec = 40.0
    elif n < 12000:
        per_sec = 18.0
    else:
        per_sec = 8.0

    maxiter = int(min(6000, max(200, int(float(seconds) * per_sec))))
    maxfun = int(max(1000, 2 * maxiter))

    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lbfgs_objective,
            a,
            args=(float(beta), float(penalty)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "ftol": 1e-18,
                "gtol": 1e-12,
                "maxls": 50,
            },
        )
        out = normalize_sequence(np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0))
        sc2 = exact_score(out)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            return out, sc2
    except Exception:
        pass

    dt = time.monotonic() - t0
    if dt > 0:
        print(f"    lbfgs beta={float(beta):.0f}: no improvement ({dt:.1f}s)")
    return best_a, best_sc


def add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, sc, label, a):
    """Update row_map and global best without nested closures."""
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return row_map, best_sc, best_a
    prev = row_map.get(label)
    if prev is None or sc < prev[0]:
        row_map[label] = (float(sc), np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy())
    if sc < best_sc:
        print(f"  NEW BEST {sc:.10f} len={int(a.size)} label={label}")
        return row_map, float(sc), np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    return row_map, best_sc, best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search routine. Returns a list of nonnegative heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2 within the available time budget.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(max(300.0, budget_s))
    usable = max(60.0, budget - 60.0)
    deadline = t0 + usable

    MAX_N = 16384

    row_map = {}
    best_sc = np.inf
    best_a = None

    # Load parent if present, but cap its length for a safe final evaluation.
    parent = None
    try:
        hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if hs is not None:
            p = normalize_sequence(hs)
            if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                if p.size > MAX_N:
                    p = resample_sequence(p, 16384)
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # ------------------------- initial starts -------------------------
    if parent is not None:
        a = normalize_sequence(parent)
        sc = exact_score(a)
        row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, sc, "parent", a)
        n_parent = int(a.size)

        # Same parent but power-of-two / nearby lengths.
        seen_lengths = {n_parent}
        for nt in [1024, 2048, 4096, 8192]:
            if nt < 512 or nt > MAX_N:
                continue
            if nt in seen_lengths:
                continue
            ar = resample_sequence(a, nt)
            if ar.size in seen_lengths:
                continue
            seen_lengths.add(ar.size)
            scar = exact_score(ar)
            row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(
                row_map, best_sc, best_a, scar, f"parent_res_{nt}", ar
            )

        nn = next_pow2(n_parent + 1)
        if 512 <= nn <= MAX_N and nn not in seen_lengths:
            ar = resample_sequence(a, nn)
            seen_lengths.add(nn)
            scar = exact_score(ar)
            row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(
                row_map, best_sc, best_a, scar, f"parent_POW2_{nn}", ar
            )

        # Asymmetric and shuffled versions of the parent help escape symmetric basins.
        if n_parent <= MAX_N:
            aa = asymmetric_start(a, rng)
            sa = exact_score(aa)
            row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(
                row_map, best_sc, best_a, sa, "parent_asym", aa
            )

        if n_parent <= 12000:
            ash = shuffled_start(a, rng)
            ssh = exact_score(ash)
            row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(
                row_map, best_sc, best_a, ssh, "parent_shuf", ash
            )

    # Generic starts, especially useful if no parent is available.
    if parent is not None:
        n_ref = n_parent if 1024 <= n_parent <= 12288 else 4096
    else:
        n_ref = 4096

    if parent is None:
        au = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n_ref, dtype=np.float64))
        scu = exact_score(au)
        row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, scu, "uniform", au)

    if len(row_map) < 20:
        q_list = [2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21]
        amp_list = [0.35, 0.85]
        added = 0
        for q in q_list:
            for amp in amp_list:
                if len(row_map) >= 20:
                    break
                phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                at = trig_start(n_ref, q, amp, phase)
                st = exact_score(at)
                label = f"trig_{q}_{int(round(amp * 100))}_{int(phase * 100)}"
                row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, st, label, at)
                added += 1
            if len(row_map) >= 20:
                break

    if len(row_map) < 24:
        an = noise_start(n_ref, rng)
        sn = exact_score(an)
        row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, sn, "noise", an)

    # Absolute fallback.
    if best_a is None:
        au = normalize_sequence(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))
        scu = exact_score(au)
        row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, scu, "fallback", au)

    print(f"Initial best: {best_sc:.10f} len={int(best_a.size)}")

    # ----------------------------- quick phase ------------------------
    t_quick_end = t0 + 0.10 * usable
    rows = [(sc, label, a) for label, (sc, a) in row_map.items()]
    rows.sort()
    quick_rows = rows[:6]

    if time.monotonic() < t_quick_end and len(quick_rows) > 0:
        qb, qw = quick_mirror_settings(int(best_a.size))
        i = 0
        while i < len(quick_rows) and time.monotonic() < t_quick_end:
            _, label, a = quick_rows[i]
            remaining_starts = max(1, len(quick_rows) - i)
            per = (t_quick_end - time.monotonic()) / float(remaining_starts)
            per = min(90.0, max(5.0, per))
            if per < 2.0:
                break
            a2, sc2 = mirror_optimize(
                a, per, rng,
                betas=qb, weights=qw,
                alpha0=0.25,
                tag=f"quick_{label}"
            )
            row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, sc2, label + "_q1", a2)
            i += 1

    # ------------------------------ main phase ------------------------
    t_main_end = t0 + 0.62 * usable
    if time.monotonic() < t_main_end:
        main_secs = max(10.0, t_main_end - time.monotonic())
        mb, mw = default_mirror_settings(int(best_a.size))
        a2, sc2 = mirror_optimize(
            best_a, main_secs, rng,
            betas=mb, weights=mw,
            alpha0=0.2,
            tag="main"
        )
        row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, sc2, "main", a2)

    # --------------------------- medium alternates --------------------
    t_med_end = t0 + 0.82 * usable
    if time.monotonic() < t_med_end:
        rows = [(sc, label, a) for label, (sc, a) in row_map.items()]
        rows.sort()
        med_candidates = []
        for sc, label, a in rows:
            if "main" in label:
                continue
            med_candidates.append((sc, label, a))
            if len(med_candidates) >= 2:
                break

        med_secs_avail = t_med_end - time.monotonic()
        if med_secs_avail > 30.0 and len(med_candidates) > 0:
            per = med_secs_avail / float(len(med_candidates))
            per = min(220.0, max(20.0, per))
            mb, mw = default_mirror_settings(int(best_a.size))
            for sc0, label, a0 in med_candidates:
                if time.monotonic() >= t_med_end:
                    break
                a2, sc2 = mirror_optimize(
                    a0, per, rng,
                    betas=mb, weights=mw,
                    alpha0=0.18,
                    tag=f"med_{label}"
                )
                row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, sc2, label + "_med", a2)
                if time.monotonic() >= t_med_end:
                    break

    # ------------------------------- final polish ---------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        sec_high = min(90.0, 0.45 * remaining)
        if sec_high > 10.0:
            hb, hw = high_mirror_settings(int(best_a.size))
            a2, sc2 = mirror_optimize(
                best_a, sec_high, rng,
                betas=hb, weights=hw,
                alpha0=0.10,
                tag="final_high_beta"
            )
            row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(row_map, best_sc, best_a, sc2, "final_high", a2)

        # L-BFGS polish, one CPU, conservative iteration budget.
        lb_betas = [2000.0, 50000.0]
        for bi, beta in enumerate(lb_betas):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 8.0:
                break
            rem = deadline - 8.0 - time.monotonic()
            s = min(120.0, rem / float(max(1, len(lb_betas) - bi)))
            if s < 10.0:
                break
            a2, sc2 = run_lbfgs_limit(best_a, beta, s, penalty=1e-4)
            row_map, best_sc, best_a = add_candidate(
                row_map, best_sc, best_a, sc2, f"lbfgs_{int(beta)}", a2
            )

    # -------------------------------- output --------------------------
    if best_a is None:
        out = [0.5] * 64
    else:
        best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(best_a.sum())
        if s < 1e-9:
            out = [0.5] * 64
        else:
            best_a = best_a / s
            if best_a.size > MAX_N:
                best_a = resample_sequence(best_a, 16384)
            out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]

    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        if not np.isfinite(verified):
            raise ValueError("invalid evaluated score")
    except Exception:
        verified = np.nan
        out = [0.5] * 64

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal best={best_sc:.10f}")
    print(f"FINAL verified={verified if np.isfinite(verified) else float('nan'):.10f}")
    print(f"Length={len(out)}, time={elapsed:.1f}s / {budget:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)
    return out
```
